(iii) 
 
 
GRADING FORMULA 
A-1   GRADE  80 % & Above  (From 880 & Above) 
A GRADE  70% to 79.99%  (From 770 to 879) 
B GRADE  60% to 69.99%  (From 660 to 769) 
C GRADE  50% to 59.99%  (From 550 to 659) 
D GRADE  40% to 49.99%  (From 440 to 549) 
E GRADE  33% to 39.99%  (From 363 to 439) 
FAIL   Below 33%  (Less than 363) 

========== PAGE 4 ==========
 
 
 
 
BOARD OF INTERMEDIATE EDUCATIONKARACHI 
NO./BIEK/CE/1048/2026      DATED:17/08/2026 
 
 
 
1. The resu lt of candidates, who appeared in the  Higher Secondary School Certificate Part -II Science                                  
Pre-Engineering Group, Annual Examination , 2026, is hereby declared today                                          
17th August, 2026  at  04:00 (pm)   This   Examination   commenced   on 30/04/2026, and the theory papers 
continued up to 08/06/2026. However practical examination continued up to 16/07/2026. 
 
2. The Result Gazette is being issued provisionally as notice only. Errors and omiss ions are expected . Any entry 
appearing therein does not in itself confer any right or privilege to a candidate for the grant of any award or 
certificate, which will be issued under the rules and regulations on the basis of original record of the Board.    
i. The Board reserves the rights of rectifying errors and omissions in the results at any time on the basis 
of original record.  
 
ii. Every care has been taken to ensure that the result is accurate. The Board however does not hold itself 
responsible for any omission or mistake of result in the Gazette. If any error or omission is noticed the 
Board may be informed immediately for necessary action. 
 
3. ISSUANCE OF MARKS CERTIFICATE.  
The marks certificates for this Examination will be issued to their respective Institut ions, within two weeks time. 
 
4. MALE CANDIDATES 
Total number of candidates registered for the Examination.   = 10762 
Total number of candidates appeared.       = 10448 
Total number of candidates passed.      =    5804 
Over all pass percentage.       =  32.22% 
  
5. FEMALE CANDIDATES 
Total number of candidates registered for the Examination.   = 7702 
Total number of candidates appeared.       = 7566 
Total number of candidates passed.      = 4118 
Over all pass percentage.       = 22.86% 
 
All those candidates, whose  results have been withheld for want of correct information/eligibility, are 
advised in their own interest to resolve these discrepancies immediately to enable the office to declare their 
results. 
 
6. UNFAIR-MEANS CASES. 
The number of Unfair -Means Cases repor ted during / after Examination were 14.  A judicious procedure is 
observed to decide all such cases. Every effort is made to provide fair opportunity of personal appearance, 
hearing and defense to all candidates involved in such cases. Results of all candi dates involved in U.F.M. are 
withheld till the final decision gets approved by the Board of Governors.  
 
 
      (ZARINA RASHID) 
    Controller of Examinations

NOTE: 
 
 
1. Any Roll Number appearing out of serial at any place in this Result release 
should be deemed as typographical error and the correct position about the 
same may be ascertained from the office. 
 
2. The Board reserves the right to correct, modify, alt er or change the Results in 
any manner if they are found to have been irregularly computed or compiled. 
 
3. Similarly, the candidature is liable to be cancelled in case of fraudulent 
appearance or concealment of facts which may come to our notice at the 
subsequent stage. 
 
4. Typographical errors, if any in this Release will not entitle any body to 
interpret the Results in his/her favour and to claim any success or advantage 
therefrom. The Board reserves the right to correct such mistakes as and when 
come to notice. 
 
5. Marks Statement will be issued  after T WO WEEKS from  their respective 
Institutions. No marks statement will at all be issued from the Board’s 
premises and no need to call at the Office of the Board for the said purpose. 
 
6. Scrutiny of Answer Book(s) can be done on payment of prescribed fee and on 
application within 40 days of the declaration of result. 
 
7. Grace Marks given (if any) are not added in the grand total. 
 
 
 
 
 
Dated:  17-08-2026                              ( ZARINA RASHID ) 
                   Controller of Examinations
